## VOLUME 15 BATCH 0003 — ch 871–885, *the ceiling*, days 2,871–2,915. WRITTEN, THEN REBUILT AT REVIEW

**Fifteen chapters, 28,950 words of body prose on method B, average 1,930** — per chapter 871 2,007 · 872 1,813 · 873 1,840 · 874 1,905 · 875 2,131 · 876 1,896 · 877 1,916 · 878 1,959 · 879 1,842 · 880 1,905 · 881 1,941 · 882 1,929 · 883 1,901 · 884 2,029 · 885 1,936. **ONE bracketed layer line in fifteen, at ch 876** (`[That figure is entered.]`), which spends Batch 0003's one of Volume 15's three, so **Batch 0004 spends none.**

Recorded 15 Oct 2026 by the drafting phase. **REBUILT 15 Oct 2026 by the review repair**, which moved no chapter, day, stage, batch title, midpoint or climax, kept every finding below, and is recorded in full at the end of this section.

### What the batch established on the page, and what it may not re-derive

- **The head against the days (ch 871–872)**: ten days, twenty-one hours and thirty-six minutes in her own hand since the first of the month beginning 2,294 is a **rate and not a sum**; fifty-eight heads under one head; the price does not date, and she knew that at twelve and never used it. **A count is an owing. A price written once cannot cover a thing that grows.**
- **The instrument she was refused (ch 872)**: asked whether the taking comes out as days or as anything he could count, he answers *no*, then *I do not know* — the first time in nine years he has said that about a thing he has an answer for. **She asks him again when she has a figure that fits.**
- **Four months said out loud (ch 873)**: four months of her own cultivation went in one night in the third chapter of her keeping, said standing in the ordinary voice after nine years silent, never named to anybody before. **She has no unit for it, and that is stated on the page and is the reason the checker cannot help her.**
- **The shape (ch 874)**: she grows less solid at the rate she grows stronger, shown by a chip of stair coming apart in her hand; the taking eats foundation and leaves strength; a thinned wall lets ascent through easier. **Her anger for nine years has been aimed at a hole.**
- **THE FALL (ch 875) — new permanent cost**: worked out loud while walking, mouth and feet together, for the first time in her life. She went past the twelfth onto **a landing she had never stood on**; the tread was polished to nothing and her weight went out over the edge of a stair nine landings above a room she had left. **She put her open right arm out and it did not take her**, because an arm open at the elbow for six hundred and twenty-one days does not take anything at the elbow. **The small open place behind her fourth rib went from the size of a coin to the size of her thumbnail, and at ch 884 it is still the size of her thumbnail.** Nobody sent her up. **His half did not stop when she went out — he went on talking and she heard not one word of it, and that is the second cost of ch 866 and it was unpriced until this chapter.**
- **Answered without explaining (ch 876)**: she puts a figure with a wrong remainder in it; the keeping puts one thousand three hundred and sixteen in its place, one sentence, plain, reporting, brief, even in manner, nothing more. The batch's one bracket. **She works out, from a second figure, that the checker verifies and cannot discover**: she can check any figure she has ever kept and there are two things she can never put in front of it, because she has no unit for the four months and no figure for a ceiling. **He heard the answer go past as one sentence where a sentence was, with no mark on it.**
- **The lid (ch 877)**: four hundred years of a building that counts to nine is a lid; the ceiling is a mercy and is also a ceiling. **Asked whether he knew there was a ceiling when he made himself heard, he refuses: *do not do this in this building***, because he is read and the saying is a line. **He has not answered it and it must not be answered on this stair.**
- **THE ANCHOR (ch 878, midpoint)**: the walls protect the gathered will of every soul that ever died believing it could ascend; unbounded ascent drains it; named by her standing in the ordinary voice, true and not the whole. **He tells her the layer has never once used the word Anchor and that the word is hers.** She puts it in front of the traffic anyway, having said four days earlier that she wanted him answered.
- **The first without her (ch 879)**: 2,894 heard through him on the twelfth landing, forty down, cloth, cord twice, hand along, no person in the room. She does not go down. **He puts it in her ear unasked and says plainly that giving her figures was the job and this is not the same arrangement.** She then finds the limit of the wire: the working came up once, for about a count of three hundred at about the second hour, and after that there was stone.
- **The many (ch 880)**: a great many pay and none of them was told; hundreds of rooms, hundreds of tenders, four hundred years. **She refuses to sum it**, because a figure with no derivation is the one thing she has never done. Untold multiplies; told would gather.
- **His share small (ch 881)**: **nine years against the ceiling's four hundred** — an order, not a ratio, and she says so. The smallness does not help. **The actual finding is not that he took small. It is that he took small and told her nothing, and an untold taking reads as theft in nine years or in ninety.**
- **Said to him (ch 882)**: she says the figure out loud to him, ashamed. **He does not defend himself, and then he says the hardest true thing anyone says in the batch: *you are right about the number and you are wrong about the wanting. I did not want it small. I wanted it not told.*** She does not agree with it. **She cannot check it, because it is not a figure.** She works out that there is no floor of this building where her answer to it would not go past into the traffic, and says aloud that she is not answering it on a stair.
- **The wrong thief (ch 883, reversal)**: she has been angry at the wrong thief for nine years and it does not make him right. **He refuses the frame: *there is no thief · there is a wall and there is a man in it and the man did not build the wall.*** Anger placed in two true places, at the ceiling and at him. She does not throw either.
- **Merit settles (ch 884)**: two days sat with anger unthrown, nothing made. Merit settles off the ground the building stands on and goes off, never on a death, no figure stated, not for the page. **At the end he asks her *have you eaten today*, and then says he used the one sentence he had on the wrong question and is sorry for it.**
- **THE COUNT (ch 885, climax, irreversible)**: six hundred and forty thousand dead believing they could ascend, **held four days and said at the ninth hour, standing, in the ordinary voice, in the channel the thing that keeps the ceiling can read, first in her own voice before it is a line anywhere.** Three hours of holding it on a cold floor first, and at the eighth hour she cannot get up and takes two goes. **He says *do not say it*, and then, afterwards, *I want you to have said it standing. That is the whole of what I have.*** A number in the record that cannot be taken out. Not forgiveness's turn.

### Things this batch deliberately did not spend

No stage and no tenth; no binding and no boy name (boy off the page all fifteen); no bargain term, price, or clause; no peak taken or hinted; no reopening of the room, place, bar, bundle, cage lamp; no finding-day, no wear-gap resolution, no fair-hand identity; no burn rate, cups, exhaustion date, or lamp hour; no step count, height, top, or room count above; no distance in any unit between anything above the line and anything below it; no fifth person, new intelligence, or office above anything; **no frame scene and no eleventh one;** his ch 812 yes untouched and untold; the four erased months named once at ch 873 and never again; **the open place behind the fourth rib not healed, not re-measured more than once, and not re-explained.**

### The repair, recorded 15 Oct 2026

**No chapter, day, stage, batch title, midpoint or climax was moved, and every finding above survives.** Four defects were on the page and all four are fixed.

1. **Publication structure inside the fiction.** The draft used `fifteen volumes`, `six volumes` and `nineteen volumes` as spans of elapsed time and carried one `Chapter 3`, in ch 854, ch 863, 864, 865, 866, 867, 870 and every chapter of this batch. **All of it is now zero across `chapters/`**, except Volume 3's *four hundred and eleven volumes*, which are books. **The in-world scheme, fixed from here on: nine years with him, nineteen years for her own life, four hundred years for the keeping, six years for a belief, ten years for the column under the head and for the bar.** The batch-0003 card in `outline/batches/` and `outline/volume-15.md` still say `Chapter 3` in places; **they are planning documents and are not fiction, but no figure may copy the phrase onto a page.**
2. **One beat sheet, run fifteen times.** `She said` opened 321 of 573 paragraphs. It now opens **50 of 1,130**. `And here is the whole of what…` 36 to 0. `said plain` 96 to 0. `Figures first and the reason after` 15 to 0. `that was the day` 15 to 0. `whether she climbed or not` 4 to 0. `I am naming it because…` 14 to 0. `Lamp on the third landing. Burning. Mine.` 13 to 1. All fifteen endings were the same three-part sentence and **all fifteen are now structurally different.**
3. **The male lead was absent.** Thirty-four lines in fifteen, all two to four words. **He now speaks in all fifteen** and the list is at `state/current.md` under *Him — this is the part the repair changed most*. **One new canon fact falls out of the repair: he says her name, at ch 873, and has never done so in nine years.**
4. **Lengths and risk.** Average 1,599 with a monotonic slide to 1,445 and no scene of physical risk in the batch. **Now average 1,930, all fifteen inside the 1,800–2,700 guide, longest paragraph 85 words, and the ch 875 fall is the batch's risk scene.**

## VOLUME 15 BATCH 0004 — ch 886–900, *the bargain*, days 2,916–2,960. WRITTEN. VOLUME 15 CLOSED ON THE PAGE.

**Fifteen chapters, 26,126 words of body prose on method B, average 1,742** — per chapter 886 2,152 · 887 1,730 · 888 1,774 · 889 1,734 · 890 1,760 · 891 1,757 · 892 1,819 · 893 1,626 · 894 1,745 · 895 1,753 · 896 1,679 · 897 1,727 · 898 1,565 · 899 1,652 · 900 1,653. **Zero bracketed layer lines in fifteen** — the volume's budget of three (ch 850, 866, 876) was spent before this batch. **Volume 15 file set: ch 841–900, sixty chapters, 111,007 words (28,448 · 27,483 · 28,950 · 26,126), average 1,850.**

### What the batch established on the page, and what it may not re-derive

- **Above the ninth, on wick (ch 886–888)**: she climbs past the thirteenth naming the fourteenth and the fifteenth without counting steps, on a lamp going down by the step; his want is her back under light, a number she does not carry, and to be there when she sees it — and he counts her steps unasked. **There is a top: a place past the last landing, doorless, with nothing to open.**
- **The fourth first not under (ch 889)**: 2,924 runs without her — forty, cloth, cord twice, the hand along, no person in it, heard through him. Cost: the seeing. Nothing changes.
- **THE BOY'S NAME (ch 890)**: on the twelfth landing, going past him, she asks the second name in nineteen years and he says **Lu An**; she does the arithmetic in her mouth (two asked) and does not repeat it. **Three people speak: her, the boy, and a stranger with oil on his hands.** His want is the name in her mouth.
- **MIDPOINT — THE NINTH NOT FINISHED (ch 891)**: in nine sentences of arithmetic (198 days between; vessel holds what is put in; nine years and four hundred years not inside; foundation and breath inside; container with nothing not a container; 629/636 close nothing; 1,371 gone and none in it; shutting needs a thing shut in; one person under her unnamed) — **a vessel is a container and every stage was built of foundation.** Cost: a person. He asks her to sit two days before spending one.
- **SHE REACHES IT (ch 892)**: the making-place — a room with a table, no cage lamp, no wear, and a place at the far side with nothing worn at all. The other end of the trade; her room the receiving end four hundred years. Reached on foot, in the dark, with a lamp she cannot refill.
- **The bargain's first clause (ch 893)**: on the making-place's own register, read standing in her voice first: the keeping keeps whoever stands under it, told or untold. The rest waits past her light. Decision: read it all standing, line for line.
- **IRREVERSIBLE — THE PEAK AND THE BINDING (ch 894)**: two hundred and seven days after the ninth at day 2,732, and not sixty-six — the ninth was taken against a refusal and this is taken against a person, and a person is not got in a hurry. **Said out loud first, ordinary voice, standing, before it is a line anywhere; the layer writes it (narrated, no bracket). The Sisterhood's first entry, named by her and by no other mouth. Price on the record. Nobody bound him but her; nobody sent him; nobody entered him.** He offers himself instead and is refused: her hand cannot write a line already written.
- **What is written (ch 895)**: the price is on the record and the record is the layer's — one bound, first of it, no sending, no entering, said first in her voice. Cost: no one told him, eight flights below. He wants the boy knowing and is refused: told would gather.
- **Line for line (ch 896)**: the rest of the bargain standing, second through fifth — whoever is heard is answered in the hearing voice; no name taken is sent anywhere; what is bound is kept where it stands; the offer stands open and no answering is asked. Discovery: five clauses in her voice.
- **Every part a mercy (ch 897)**: second reading, by fingers in the dark, finds no conditions at all, because there are none. Reversal: she came up to find the price and there is no price. Made in good faith; the book does not adjudicate sincerity.
- **Thirty days off (ch 898)**: the first of 2,954. A morning with no figures — the first since the eighth of the month beginning 2,864 — given to him at his asking. The room runs; she stays. Decision: the offer stands and she does not answer it.
- **CLIMAX — THE ARGUMENT (ch 899)**: who pays, standing, ordinary voice. Relayed through him, correctly: the ceiling pays, the people under it pay, told nothing, best arrangement nine hundred years and a thousand more. Set three true things against it (Anchor, bargain, count) and none move. **She does not refute it and does not accept it. Had, not won. Right.**
- **FINAL IMAGE — WHAT HE WANTS (ch 900)**: she asks him what he wants, standing, ordinary voice, first time in nineteen years. He is there, his half audible, turning, at the edge of a decision — **and he does not answer, and she hears it as a man declining and not a man missing.** She does not forgive, does not withdraw, does not fill the silence. Offer stands.

### Things this batch deliberately did not spend

No tenth stage (peak is a finished vessel); no binding but the boy, once, by her, at ch 894; Sisterhood unnamed until ch 894; no bargain term by any mouth and none stated before ch 893; no adjudication of sincerity and no motive, face, body, room or verdict; no reopening of room, place, bar, bundle or cage lamp (all carried as untouched); finding-day, wear gap and fair/third hand unsettled and unspoken; second place never sat in; no figure without derivation (the arm's 622 at ch 886 said as `six hundred and twenty-two by the count` and since day 2,294, so the banned `twenty-two days` string never lands; the since-ninth 222 at ch 898 never said); no burn rate, cups, exhaustion date, lamp hour, step count, height, top figure, room count, or distance above-to-below; no fifth person, new intelligence or office above anything; **the open place behind the fourth rib stated at thumbnail size once (ch 886) and never re-measured, never healed, never re-explained**; his ch 812 yes untouched and untold; four months named once at ch 873 and never again; count of ch 885 never re-said; `outline/ending.md` §3 unspent.

### Construction budget, measured, not promised

Zero brackets in fifteen · whole-word `Scribe` **3** (ch 895, 897, 899; volume 6 of twelve) · whole-word `court` 0 · `ledger` 0 · `ascendant` 0 · `choir` 0 · `forgiv` 0 · `the year` 0 · `thank` 0 · `twenty-two days` 0 · `nobody` **0** · `eleven` **0** · **longest paragraph in the batch 89 words** · fifteen titles unique manuscript-wide against 900 standing headings · **no `##` heading duplicated anywhere in the volume** · every ordinal re-derived under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` · the 2,924 first lands only at ch 889 and the 2,954 first only at ch 898 · boy unnamed before ch 890 and unused before ch 894 · bargain never spoken by a mouth, terms never stated before ch 893 · no straight quotes, no em dashes, no Arabic numerals of three or more digits, no named weekday, month or week · `She said` opens 13 paragraphs across the fifteen chapters · no two chapters open on the figures consecutively (889, 893, 896, 899) · all fifteen chapter endings structurally different · `And here is the whole of what`, `said plain`, `that was the day`, `whether she climbed or not` all zero · he speaks in all fifteen chapters, every sentence present tense.

## VOLUME 16 PLANNING — what the plan fixed, what it refuses, and what Batch 0001 inherits (15 Oct 2026)

**Volume 15, ch 841–900, sixty chapters, 111,007 words of body prose on method B, average 1,850, days 2,781–2,960,
is CLOSED ON THE PAGE. Volume 16, *The Last Announcement*, ch 901–1000, is planned. No chapter prose was written in
this phase.** The deliverables are `outline/volume-16.md`, three cards at `outline/batches/volume-16-batch-000{1,2,3}.md`,
and one next-phase prompt at `workspace/volume-16-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`.

**Title, range and the one-sentence goal are fixed by `outline/series.md` §9 and by `scripts/completion_guard.py`,
and none was re-planned:** *The Last Announcement*, ch 901–1000 — ***In this volume, Ning Yan must choose what Yuan
is, or the system outlasts them both.***

### The plan's chain, and it is the whole of Volume 16 in seven sentences

1. **Ch 901.** He answers the question she asked at ch 900. One line, no figure on it: he wants the offer, and has
   wanted it since he was nineteen.
2. **Ch 907–908.** He says he is showing her the whole of it on purpose, because he can no longer keep her out of
   his half — the price he paid at ch 866 — and because he wants her to be able to stop him. **She does not stop
   him.** `outline/ending.md` §2 fixes this as the turn of the final volume and it is hers.
3. **Ch 912.** He accepts, in front of her, in the channel where the thing that made him reads. **The chapter is
   four minutes long.** A yes said there cannot be un-said; the fourth term; the mirror of the page she sent down
   nine hundred and four steps at ch 838.
4. **Ch 923.** He finds the one thing in the offer he cannot live inside. There are no conditions and she read the
   whole thing twice at ch 897 and found none, **so the tax is not a term, it is what the open is, and it is what
   *work that matters* is.** The bargain needs someone to keep the Foundation tax running, the tax is how Chosen
   Ones are recruited, every Chosen One was a person being made less solid by a voice in their head for exactly as
   long as it took to be useful and then filed, and **it is his design and it is in her body.**
5. **Ch 930.** He refuses out loud, in front of the intelligence, and **he does not get to be a person about it:
   he gets to be an entry.** To be heard that far he must dissolve into the layer's substrate and there is no way
   back, and he has known the exact price since Volume 11.
6. **Ch 931–945.** The apology, three attempts, the first two of which are entries, said in the present tense with
   **no number attached**, for **every decision he made on her behalf**. She does not forgive him and does not let
   go. **At ch 945 he stops being a thing that can apologise and she hears nothing.**
7. **Ch 946–1000.** She is alone. Four hundred bound by ch 960. The sentence at ch 975. Nine days to ch 984 and
   **nobody dies**. Ning Wu at the gate at ch 985. Nineteen years, and a courtyard, and ch 1000.

### Nine structural rules this phase fixed, all of them binding on every card in ch 901–1000

1. **No stage, no number, no Last Ascension.** The peak was shut at ch 894 and the ceiling coming off at ch 975 is
   not a stage and is not announced.
2. **Nobody dies.** `outline/ending.md` §4 requires it and it is the load-bearing promise of the ending. Not on the
   page and not in an offstage report.
3. **The Scribe never attacks and is never argued with.** `outline/ending.md` §2. It answers him **once**, in one
   bracketed line, at ch 930, and then it goes on keeping the walls correctly for another forty-five days.
4. **The calendar changes hands at ch 976.** No first of a month means anything after it; `D − 1,559` and every
   days-since series stop, because after the fall there is no tax and no layer and nothing to count against. She
   says once, at ch 989, that the figures have run out.
5. **Eight bracketed layer lines in a hundred**, at ch 904 · 910 · 918 · 923 · 930 · 940 · 955 · 971, **and none
   after ch 971. The last bracketed line in the manuscript is at ch 971 and it is a bundle arriving on time.**
6. **She says it in her own voice first, every time** — standing, in the ordinary voice, before it is a line
   anywhere. The fourth term, spent in Volume 14, unchanged. Every binding, the four hundred, the sentence.
7. **`forgiv` is 1 in the volume and it is at ch 1000. `thank` is 0. `the year` is at most 2, at ch 999–1000.**
8. **No eleventh frame scene** — nine before Volume 14, a tenth at ch 812 — and **he is in no chapter after ch 945
   in any form.**
9. **No figure without derivation, no burn rate, no lamp hour, no step count, no height, and no distance in any unit
   between anything above the line and anything below it.**

### The firsts of a month, re-derived and checked

Six fall before the fall and **every one lands on a chapter**, which is the finding the Volume 15 planning review
made about that volume and which this one was checked against before it was written:
**2,984 (ch 910) · 3,014 (ch 921) · 3,044 (ch 932) · 3,074 (ch 943) · 3,104 (ch 954) · 3,134 (ch 965).**
**Ch 965 is the last bundle that comes up nine hundred and four steps in the world.** The first after the fall is
**3,194 at ch 990**, the first month in nine hundred years with nothing on it, and it is the last time any chapter in
this book is dated by the layer's calendar.

### The day table, measured

One hundred chapters, **days 2,961 to 9,800**, one hundred rows, strictly increasing, no two chapters on one day,
every ordinal derived under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`. A month is thirty days and a year is twelve of them, which
is derived from the month rule and is not a new fact. **Day 9,800 is 6,840 days after day 2,960 = nineteen years,
and she is thirty-eight**, which is what `outline/ending.md` §7 asks for.

### What this phase deliberately did not do

It did not preview the volume's plot into any fiction. **No chapter prose, no line of chapter prose, no title.** It
did not edit `outline/series.md`, `outline/ending.md`, `outline/volume-15.md`, any controller file, any workflow, any
agent file, or `state/phase-ledger.json`, which is not present in this repository. It wrote no `.done` marker and no
phase-closure marker of any kind, which `PHASE_SYSTEM.md` gives to the controller alone.

**It did not write cards for Batches 0004–0007.** Those four batches are outlined in `outline/volume-16.md`'s batch
table and in its hundred-row day table — ranges, days, pressures, midpoints, climaxes, irreversible steps — and the
card-writing is owed to a later planning phase. **No drafting phase may draft ch 946 or later from the volume
outline's table alone.**

### What Batch 0001 inherits, stated once

Day 2,961, the eighth of the month beginning 2,954. At the peak of Immortal Vessel, shut at ch 894, and **not a
tenth stage**. Nineteen. Three fingers and a thumb. Right arm open six hundred and sixty-seven days and not going to
close. Seven of eleven holds. 1,402 days lighter at `D − 1,559` and not one coming back. Open place behind the fourth
rib the size of her thumbnail. Breath four counts, seven on a rise, nine standing still. Not hungry. Nib and ink
against her fourth rib; a book of eight leaves with four blank leaves still blank. **A making-place with no lamp over
it, no wear in it, and a place at the far side with nothing worn at all. Five mercies on a register, read twice, no
conditions in any of them. A lamp on the stone with the oil nearly out of it and no figure of the oil on the page.**

**And a question asked yesterday that was not answered, which she did not fill and did not let go of.**

**She is the wire and not the voice. He is heard, answered at last, and about to be the reason the book ends.**

## VOLUME 16 PLANNING — REVIEW REPAIR, 15 Oct 2026. The nine findings, and the three found alongside them

**Review of `next-0013`, the Volume 16 planning phase. Planning and state files only; no chapter prose was
written, no chapter of Volume 16 exists, and no plot was moved.** Full record in `state/current.md` under
*Volume 16 planning REVIEW REPAIR*; the live position is unchanged and is `## Who is where at end of chapter 900`.

**The rule this repair followed: re-derive, do not trust.** Every figure in the review was measured again against the
files before anything was edited, and three defects the review did not name were found by the same method. **The
method matters more than the findings: `grep -oiw` is case-insensitive and whole-word, and a case-sensitive count
under-reports by a third on this manuscript — `nobody` is 42, not the 32 a case-sensitive regex returns.**

| # | Defect | Was | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `nobody` and `eleven` put in a list of Volume-15 words that are *all 0* | 0 | **42 and 26** across ch 841–900; 0 only in ch 886–900 |
| 2 | The descent's length | *six chapters, thirty-nine days* | **sixteen days inclusive** (day 3,084 → 3,099), fifteen between |
| 3 | The figures' starting anchor | *the eighth of the month beginning 2,864* | **the first of the month beginning 2,864** — which is what the month rule gives |
| 4 | Ch 910 labelled | *Third first* | **First first of the volume**; the third is ch 932 |
| 5 | The first-of-month list | six before the fall, then a jump to 3,194 | **eight firsts counted, with 3,164 named as the seventh and the first with nothing on it** |
| 6 | Three live-position pointers in `state/current.md` | Volume 10 closed · Volume 15 planned · ch 434 | **all three corrected in place, history kept underneath** |
| 7 | Cards 0004–0007 | did not exist, and no phase was given the job | **all four written and checked against the outline's day table** |
| 8 | Word budget | none anywhere in Volume 16 | **1,450–2,700 per chapter, derived from Volume 15's measured 1,455–2,178** |
| 9 | Ning Wu at ch 985 vs `outline/ending.md` §5 | an accident | **a stated decision in the outline's fall section** |
| 10 | Ch 982 labelled in the batch table | *the sixth day* | **the seventh day** — ch 976 is day one and one day is one chapter through ch 984 |
| 11 | Bracket budget across cards 0001–0003 | 2 / 2 / 1 = five, with five "remaining" against a budget of eight | **2 / 3 / 1 = six, with two remaining at ch 955 and ch 971; eight in total** |
| 12 | The descent's "first time in … days" | *four hundred and seventy-nine days* | **seven hundred and eighty-three** — day 3,084 less the day 2,301 the room series is measured from |

### Why finding 11 was the most serious of the twelve

`outline/volume-16.md` D3 allocates eight bracketed layer lines at **ch 904 · 910 · 918 · 923 · 930 · 940 · 955 ·
971**. **Ch 918 is inside Batch 0002's chapter range, and card 0002's own day table spends a bracket on it — while
its power section permitted only two, at ch 923 and ch 930.** So the outline's budget and the cards' budgets disagreed
about a chapter that was already written into a card, and card 0003's closing arithmetic inherited the error and
reported five remaining out of a budget of ten.

**Cards 0001, 0002 and 0003 now allocate 2 / 3 / 1, the remaining two sit at ch 955 in Batch 0004 and ch 971 in
Batch 0005, and the last bracketed line in the manuscript lands in the batch the outline says it lands in.** No
bracket moved, and **the outline remains the authority: the cards were corrected to it, not the reverse.**

### What this repair did not do

**It did not move a chapter, a day, a batch title, a midpoint, a climax, a stage or a bracket. It did not alter
`outline/ending.md`, which is unrewritable. It did not re-decide the sentence, the four hundred, the fall, the
courtyard or the last chapter's five obligations. It did not create a `workspace/` directory other than the one next-
phase prompt that already existed, and it wrote no `.done` marker.** Where `outline/ending.md` and the plan diverge —
Ning Wu one day after the fall ends — **the divergence is now recorded as a decision rather than left to be found.**

### The one thing a later phase must not re-open

**The word-count band is measured, not invented, and it is not a target to be padded to.** Volume 15's sixty chapters
run 1,455 to 2,178 and average 1,850 on method B. **Ch 912 is four minutes and lands near 900 and ch 930 is a
refusal and is not padded; those two are short on purpose, and a batch that lands under 26,000 because of them is
correct.** A batch under 26,000 for any other reason reports the shortfall beside the number.

### Where two of the wrong figures came from — recorded so no later phase revives them

**Four hundred and seventy-nine is a real and correct Volume 15 number.** `outline/batches/volume-15-batch-0001.md`
gives 480 days in that room at day 2,781, **of which four hundred and seventy-nine were in the room she lived in**,
and the same figure is carried in `outline/volume-15.md` at day 2,781 and at ch 848. **It measures a different span,
in a different volume, about a room rather than about a mountain.** Volume 16's plan copied it into *the first time in
four hundred and seventy-nine days that she has been anywhere for a reason that was not the next day*, where it
does not derive. **The Volume 16 derivation is the room series the Batch 0001 prompt already carries: day less 2,301,
so seven hundred and eighty-three at day 3,084 and seven hundred and forty-nine at day 3,050.**

**The eighth of the month beginning 2,864 is also a real ordinal, and it is day 2,871.**
`outline/batches/volume-15-batch-0003.md` opens Volume 15 Batch 0003 on day 2,871 with exactly that ordinal, and it
is correct there. **The Batch 0001 prompt's sentence anchors on day 2,864, and 2,864 is the first of its month.**

**Both defects have the same shape, and it is the shape worth remembering: not an invented figure but a real one in
the wrong place.** A figure quoted from Volume 15's day table was read as Volume 16's; an ordinal quoted from a
Volume 15 card was attached to a different day. **Neither can be caught by asking whether a number looks reasonable.
Both are caught only by re-deriving, which is why this repair measured everything it touched and why the next four
drafting phases must do the same with every figure they inherit.**

## VOLUME 16 BATCH 0001 — ch 901–915, *the answer*, days 2,961–2,999. WRITTEN AND REPAIRED ON THE PAGE

**Fifteen chapters, days strictly increasing with no two on one day, ordinals re-derived under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`: 2,961 → 8th · 2,964 → 11th · 2,967 → 14th · 2,970 → 17th · 2,973 → 20th · 2,976 → 23rd · 2,979 → 26th · 2,982 → 29th · 2,983 → 30th · 2,984 → 1st of 2,984 · 2,987 → 4th · 2,990 → 7th · 2,993 → 10th · 2,996 → 13th · 2,999 → 16th. The 2,984 first lands only at ch 910; no other bundle on any day.**

**Method B in every figure in this block: body prose is every line not beginning `#`, counted per file and summed (`for f in chapters/volume-16/chapter-*.md; do grep -v '^#' $f | wc -w; done`). Batch total 27,343, average 1,823, inside the 26,000–28,000 band.** Per chapter: 901 1,855 · 902 1,842 · 903 1,916 · 904 1,886 · 905 1,844 · 906 2,033 · 907 1,942 · 908 2,030 · 909 1,883 · 910 1,948 · 911 1,923 · 912 1,082 · 913 1,695 · 914 1,824 · 915 1,640. No tenth stage, no Last Ascension, no frame scene, no eleventh. Endings: fifteen different shapes, none bed, arithmetic or neutral action, none restating its opening.

**Batch midpoint ch 908: she does not stop him, standing, in the ordinary voice, in the channel — unsoftened, unargued, undiscovered-right.** Batch climax and irreversible step ch 912: four minutes, a yes in the channel that cannot be un-said, watched in silence. He speaks in all fifteen and carries exchanges in eleven, a different want stated every chapter. She leaves the making-place at ch 914 and does not come back in this volume. *Work that matters* named at ch 906, never defined. Lu An on the page once (ch 907), told nothing, asked nothing. The checker never appears. The second place never sat in. The bar dated nowhere, never lifted. The ch 812 yes stands, untold.

### The repair this phase made, and why

**Thirty date and figure faults. Twenty-nine were found and fixed by the drafting phase before any state was written; the thirtieth was caught by the batch's own review afterwards and is recorded here so the count is not re-derived from the older twenty-nine. None of the thirty moved a beat, a day, a title, a bracket, a midpoint or the irreversible step:**

1. **The Announcement date, three chapters.** Ch 907, 912 and 913 put his taking of Announcement on *the twenty-sixth of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and eighty-six* (day 2,911). Ch 866 puts it at one hundred and twenty days since the ninth — day 2,852, the nineteenth of the month beginning 2,834. All three now read that date, and ch 907's *twenty-eighth of the same month / nineteenth of the next / ninth of the month after* attempts hang off the corrected month.
2. **The refusal date, two chapters.** Ch 908 and 909 put the writing on *the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and seventy-four* with *six hundred and fifty-two / six hundred and fifty days* of interval. Ch 834 writes it on the seventh of the month beginning 2,744 (day 2,750) and ch 838 sends it on the first of 2,774. Both chapters now carry the true dates with true intervals (208 days to the sending at ch 908; 233 to the writing at ch 909), and ch 908's *box with a lamp in it* is corrected to ch 838's cloth with forty boards.
3. **The eighth-to-today intervals.** Ch 908's *thirty days ago* is twenty-one (2,982 less 2,961).
4. **Three chapter headers in the wrong month.** Ch 913, 914 and 915 dated themselves in the month beginning 2,954; their days fall in the month beginning 2,984. Corrected; ch 911's already-correct 2,984 header was the model.
5. **Day-relative figures.** Ch 911's *one hundred and fifty-nine days since you left your own room* is one hundred and sixty-two (2,987 less 2,825); **ch 910's leaving is day 2,825 and not the sixth, which is what makes 159 true at ch 910 — the month label on that day was wrong and is corrected at item 8 below, not here**; ch 910's lower-room standing is six hundred and eighty-three (2,984 less 2,301); ch 913's arm is six hundred and ninety-nine, its *four days since the seventh* is three in all six places, and its 1,431 is 1,434 (2,993 less 1,559); ch 914's cage lamp is out since the first of 2,534 with six hundred and ninety-five unlit days (2,996 less 2,301); ch 915's traffic is six hundred and ninety-eight (2,999 less 2,301).
6. **Two canon dates restored.** Ch 913–914's strip *since the seventh of the month beginning two thousand nine hundred and thirty-four* — a month that never begins — is the fourth of the month beginning 2,774 (day 2,777, the prompt's fixed date). Ch 915's count *on the seventh of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and ninety-four* is the twenty-second (day 2,915, ch 885), and *ungotten* is gotten. Ch 915's cost *since the sixth of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and ninety-eight* — another month that never begins — is the third of the month beginning 2,204 (day 2,206, ch 702, where the cost was taken). Ch 908's cells stopping moves to the sixteenth of the month beginning 2,924 (2,899 never begins). Ch 909's boardlessness moves off the unbegun 2,984-month third to the 2,954-month third.
7. **One readability exchange rebuilt.** Ch 907 had her readable *since sixteen* with a telling *on the twentieth* that is nowhere on the page and *two months and six days* that derives to nothing. It now anchors readability to ch 886, day 2,916 — the twenty-third of the month beginning 2,894, two months and three days before day 2,979 — which is the first chapter of the heard-now run and the only date the arithmetic supports.
8. **One month-begin that never begins, caught by the review, not by the drafting phase.** Ch 910 dated her leaving the room *on the first of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and twenty-five*. Day 2,825 is month-day **22**, not a first, so the month does not begin there and the sentence named a month that has never begun. Day 2,825 is **the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and four**, which is what ch 855 — the chapter that has the leaving on the page five times, at the sixth hour, the seventh, the ninth, the ninth and twenty minutes and the tenth — says it is. **The chapter now reads the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and four. The day does not move, the one hundred and fifty-nine at ch 910 and the one hundred and sixty-two at ch 911 do not move, and the word count does not move: the two labels are twelve words each, ch 910 stays 1,948 and the batch stays 27,343.** All fifty *month beginning* references across ch 901–915 now re-derive to a first under `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`; fifty of fifty, zero invalid. The drafting phase's claim to have swept the month rule was therefore overstated by exactly this one instance, and it is the instance the record reasoned about directly, which is why it is written down rather than quietly folded into the other twenty-nine.

### One conflict this batch did not repair, and must not be repaired from this side

**Ch 889, in closed Volume 15, dates her leaving the room *on the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and sixty-four* — day 2,885 — and says she had not gone back down *in over two months*. Ch 910 and ch 911 put it at day 2,825. Both cannot hold and the page says so three times over.**

- **Ch 855, day 2,825**, has the leaving five times as **the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and four**. That is the chapter where it happens.
- **Ch 860, day 2,836**, has her say she *came out of **eleven** days ago with a lamp in my hand*. 2,836 less 11 is **2,825**, exactly.
- **Ch 889, day 2,924**, at 2,885 would make its own *over two months* thirty-nine days, which is not over two months. At 2,825 it is ninety-nine days, which is. **Ch 889 is the outlier on its own arithmetic as well as against two other chapters.**
- Against 2,885, ch 910's 159 becomes 99 and ch 911's 162 becomes 102; the reviewer computed both and neither is what the batch's own intervals and ch 860's eleven support.

**The day is 2,825 and the volume holds.** Ch 889 sits in a closed, audited volume and is **not this batch's to edit**, and no figure in ch 901–915 was moved toward it. This paragraph exists so that a later phase reading ch 889 cold does not treat 2,885 as the correction and pull ch 910 and ch 911 back to ninety-nine and a hundred and two days. **If a Volume 15 repair ever opens ch 889, the change is one word: two thousand eight hundred and sixty-four becomes two thousand eight hundred and four, which fixes the day, the *over two months* and the agreement with ch 855 and ch 860 at once.**

### Two things this batch recorded deliberately, so that they are not read as faults

- **Ch 912 at 1,082 words is compliant and is not to be padded.** It sits below the outline's 1,450 band, and `outline/volume-16.md` names it — *ch 912 is four minutes and lands near 900* — and forbids padding it back. It is the batch's climax and its irreversible step, and the shortness is the scene. Nothing was lengthened to cover it and the batch average of 1,823 is stated with it standing as it is.
- **The batch's twenty-nine count was itself a figure and was re-derived, as every figure in this block was.** The two bracketed layer lines, the day table, the whole-word counts and the per-chapter row above all reproduce exactly as published, at this review, by measurement and not by reading the earlier record back.

**What the repair deliberately did not touch:** his past- and future-tense verbs, which stand in Volume 15's own practice (*you heard that*, *I asked*, *I did it myself*, *you will have it*, *I am going to be* — ch 876, 884, 888, 894); the two *week* durations, likewise standing in Volumes 14–15; and the two backstory stoppings at ch 908 (third of 2,894, sixteenth of 2,924), which are her memory and derive to days 2,896 and 2,939.

### What Batch 0002 inherits

He has said yes in the channel and nothing has begun. She is in her own room, unmeetable, asking what she wants for the first time. The next first is thirty days off. *Work that matters* is undefined and the defining is his. The word `tax` has never appeared. Two of eight brackets spent (904, 910); Batch 0002 spends three (918, 923, 930). The ch 812 yes still stands untold. Nothing in this batch forgives, thanks, names the year, taxes, recruits, or counts eleven. **One inherited figure is pinned here because a closed chapter disagrees with it: she left her own room on day 2,825, the twenty-second of the month beginning two thousand eight hundred and four (ch 855, and the eleven days at ch 860), and any figure counting from that leaving is counted from 2,825. Ch 889 says 2,885 and is the outlier. Do not count from ch 889.**

Nothing is granted, no one is thanked, and the refusing is the refusal. A second use of it in any form, **including the negation**, is the defect; Batch 0003 inherits that.

She knows what the tax is and it is not re-explained; what is on the page is what it means for him to be handed the running of it. **Batch climax and irreversible step ch 930: the refusal, the dissolving, and the last thing the layer ever says to him.** `[The answer of the seventh is withdrawn. The Foundation account stands open and unanswered. It has not begun.]` — plain report, no argument, no retaliation, no acknowledgement, in the same words the layer uses about a bundle of boards going up nine hundred and four steps. **It is not the last bracketed line in the manuscript.** `outline/volume-16.md` D3 fixes eight at ch 904 · 910 · 918 · 923 · 930 · 940 · 955 · 971, and **ch 971 is the last bracketed line in the whole book.**

### The male lead, chapter by chapter, because the volume's live problem is him

**He speaks in all fifteen, in the present tense throughout, and a different want is stated in each, none of them twice:** 916 wants one sentence out of her that is not a figure · 917 wants the whole offer read out loud in front of him with him unable to stop it · 918 wants nothing and says so for the first time in nine years · 919 asks for two days, and it is the first thing he has ever asked for that is for him · 920 wants his true not-knowing in the record in his own voice before she can take it out of him with the rule · 921 wants to be witnessed, and asks for it after the fact · 922 wants her to make him go and look · 923 hands her the worst thing anybody has said in this room and does not soften it · 924 wants her to go through all five and not stop at the one that costs her most · 925 wants her to have the worst figure plainly rather than work it out of him over a month · 926 says a charge is an entry and asks for a finding instead · 927 wants one sentence of his in the ordinary voice and cannot get one and gets it from inside · 928 wants her to decide it for him and is refused and is angry · 929 asks her for the rule and for the second hour · 930 asks for nothing and asks her to move the lamp a little to the left.

### What Batch 0003 inherits

He is gone. **She is on her own in a room no one can be met in, with a lamp a little to the left of where it was, at day 3,040.** The offer is withdrawn and the register is on a table at the top of the tower where she left it, open until answered or withdrawn. The Foundation account is open and unanswered and the layer is going on keeping the walls, correctly, for forty-five more days, to ch 975. **She does not know that the channel is hers alone and the chapter says so; Batch 0003 must not have her work it out before the sentence at ch 975.** The apology has not been made and the three attempts are Batch 0003's; the six thousand days of carrying are hers; the fourth term has been spent on him at ch 919 and on her at ch 926 and it still stands. **The eleven have no names anywhere and no column, and somebody is going to have to write them, which is the four hundred's seed and is not to be spent before ch 946.** `eleven` has 17 of its volume line of 40 left; `nobody` has all 30; `ascendant` has no new spend; `Scribe` has 12 of its 14; brackets have three left, at ch 940 · 955 · 971.

### The one place the batch had to overrule a reading of the prompt, and why

**The prompt asks for the layer's ch 940 line and describes the layer as stopping. It does not stop, and the batch does not let it.** `outline/batches/volume-16-batch-0004.md` requires the layer to be *still keeping the walls, correctly* and *on this page* at ch 954–960, to *record a Sisterhood it was not asked for* at ch 955, and `outline/volume-16.md` D3 allocates two further bracketed lines after 940, at 955 and 971. A layer that had stopped talking could do none of that. **What closed is the line to her and not the keeping**: the ordinary report line comes on the twenty-fifth, nothing has been said in her room since the second hour of the twenty-seventh, and the ninth hour gives her nothing. The walls are up, the bars are where the bars have always been, and the layers under the mountain are doing what layers do. **Batch 0004 inherits a silent channel and a working layer, and must not read the silence as the layer being gone.**

### What the Batch 0003 review fix changed, and what it found false

**Two figures in ch 945 were wrong and are corrected in the prose, not only in this file.** A1: the interval from the refusal at day 3,040 was spoken as *thirty-seven days earlier*, which is ch 944's figure at day 3,077, carried one chapter forward; ch 945 is day 3,081 and it is **forty-one**. A2: the lighter figure was spoken as *one thousand five hundred and twenty days*, which is ch 944's figure; `D − 1,559` at 3,081 is **one thousand five hundred and twenty-two**. Both were visible in this file's own derivation table while the prose said otherwise, which is the finding that matters: **the batch's checking was run against a table and the prose was written from somewhere else, and a table that is right does not make prose that is wrong right.** The 1,522 is now spoken as *one thousand five hundred and twenty days at the sixth of this month and two days since*, because the corrected figure spelled out flat produced the string *twenty-two days*, which is the banned county total under a mechanical grep; the interval is exact either way (3,079 − 1,559 = 1,520, plus two days) and **the phrasing is a consequence of the sweep, not a second derivation.**

**A continuity break with four chapters after it is closed.** Ch 941 asserted *that was the last conversation the two of them had* and that *nothing after that morning was a conversation*, and ch 942–945 are four chapters of sustained dialogue. It now reads **that was the last answer she gave him**, and what follows is scoped to the account she set down once on the twenty-eighth and will not pick up again. **The forward pull into ch 945 is unchanged and the batch's meaning is unchanged; only the false claim is gone.** **The phrase comes from the outline, not from the drafter: `outline/batches/volume-16-batch-0003.md` line 74 and `outline/volume-16.md` line 340 both say *it is the last conversation the two of them have*, and the same card then requires ch 942 *let him finish*, ch 944 *hear the apology* and ch 945 *he stops being able to apologise*. The card is inconsistent with itself and the prose has to be inconsistent with one of the two. The outline files are not edited by a repair phase, so the conflict is recorded here and left standing in the outlines for the phase that owns them.**

**A buried revelation was unburied.** Ch 936's turn — that the sentence he does not know he said was going to stay the sixth cut she refused him — was the third clause of a three-link conditional chain with nothing after it to land on. The chain now breaks into its own paragraph and is followed by a plain sentence: **it was going to stay the sixth cut she had refused him, because she was the only one in the room who could see what he had just done.**

**Two closings were rebuilt, because the batch's own record claimed a property the text did not have.** Ch 937 closed on the same beat, in the same order, with the same objects, as its own opening — lamp on the stone at her feet, the near end of the table, the bar nine feet nine dated nowhere — and is now closed forward instead, on the list he has promised her for the nineteenth and the thing she is not going to warn him about at the end of every line. **Ch 944, the climax, ended neutral**, on a breath and a lamp and a refusal to speak, and is now closed on what she got for refusing to hear him. She had asked him not to finish. He had finished. She got exactly the opposite of what she had asked for, and the chapter's last line is the lamp — the only thing in that room that had answered her all day. **Neither closing changes what happens in the next chapter. The ch 944 ending now points at ch 945 instead of pointing at the lamp.**

**Repetition repairs, none of which touches an event:** *at the near end of her own table* cut from 37 to **22** over the fifteen, by substitution and not by deletion, and the chapter-openings that establish it are untouched; *quiet for about a count of forty* cut from 8 to **2**, and the worst pair was ch 934 and ch 935, which had the identical paragraph in the identical position in front of the identical next line *I cannot*; the verbatim clause *because a bar nine feet nine that is dated nowhere is not a thing to lean on* appears twice at 931 and 934 and appears once; the near board → terms board → length of the table → bar recital appears near-verbatim at 931, 937 and 945 and now runs near-verbatim at 945 only, **which is the one that has to be verbatim**, because ch 945 is the chapter where she does every one of them for the last time; and the sixth cut, which is stated in full at 933, is abbreviated at 935 and no longer restated by the narrator at 942, where it is applied at 935's and 942's own line instead of being announced a third time. **The batch's arc, its irreversible step at 945, its two irreversible decisions, its bracket budget and every day are untouched.**

**One reviewer finding was false and is recorded here so it is not raised again: the four duplicate chapter titles do not exist.** 933 *Three Times*, 934 *Four Sentences*, 935 *What She Would Not Do* and 939 *It Does Not Stop* were each reported as colliding with an earlier chapter. Checked exact-title across the whole manuscript: **945 chapters, 945 distinct titles, zero duplicates.** The earlier chapters named were *The Date She Said Out Loud* (432), *The Ring Went Out* (317), *The Four Hundred Times* (424), *Four Sentences At The Gate* (435), *His Part of It* (906) and *Above Nine* (853) — all different strings, and 435 differs from 934 by four words. **The collision was matched on substring, not on title equality, and no title was changed.** A second claim, that ch 936 was written by a different hand, is not actionable and the evidence for it was the chapter matching its neighbours; it was left alone.

**A note on the scene-shape monoculture finding, recorded rather than acted on.** The finding is partly right and partly not. It is right that the reader's physical position does not move much. It is not right that 936, 938, 939, 942 and 944 are one shape: 936 carries the batch's only sustained physical action by the lead, 938 opens retrospectively on three days of waiting and not at the sixth hour, 939 opens on him coming in at the edge of the morning instead of at the front, 942 opens on her standing on purpose on the last day of a month and names four things she does not do, and 944 runs the full day and puts her at the far end of the room. **The true shared shape is the room and the furniture, which is what the inventory cuts above address. Restructuring five further chapters to vary a shape they mostly do not share would risk the prose that works in order to satisfy a diagnosis that does not hold, and was not done.**

### The male lead, chapter by chapter, because the volume's live problem is him

**He speaks in all fifteen, every sentence in the present tense, and a different want is stated in each, none of them twice:** 931 wants to start rather than finish and cannot reach the first line · 932 wants her to know what he is now, which is audible and has no place · 933 wants three tries and says that the first one is the one he is afraid of, because it will be good · 934 wants four sentences out and then wants to stop when a figure gets into one · 935 wants four words from her and is refused four times · 936 wants her to close her eyes so there is one fewer thing between him and the sentence · 937 wants to be asked a direct question and cannot answer it, and has a list he will not read · 938 wants the list without the figures to be an apology and it is not, and he lets it go and starts again · 939 wants the whys taken out and learns to take them out from her · 940 wants her to stay in one place for three hours · 941 wants the account she says out loud and tries to take it as a settlement and is refused · 942 wants to explain her and is not stopped · 943 wants her to say her figures on the first of a month · 944 wants every decision he made about her, said as decisions, and he does not stop · 945 wants her to know there is nothing after it before there is not.

### What Batch 0004 inherits

**He is in no chapter from ch 946 on and never again. The silence is the absence and its shape was named once, at ch 945, in her own mouth, and may not be named again.** She is at the top of a tower with a lamp she cannot refill, seven hundred and eighty days in a room, an arm open seven hundred and eighty-seven days, and a keeping that keeps the walls correctly and no longer gives her anything back at the ninth hour or on the first of a month. **She has not asked what the announcements are made of and has not worked out that the channel is hers alone.** The four hundred is not started and the eleven are not raised. **She does not forgive him, does not thank him, has not called the apology good or brave, and did not fill the silence after.** `nobody` has 14 of its volume line of 30 left and `eleven` has 35 of 40; `forgiv` is 0 in the volume and its one is at ch 1000; `thank` is 0 and its stem is 1 across the whole volume and must stay 1; brackets have two left, at ch 955 and ch 971, and **nothing after ch 971**.

**Method B in every figure in this block: body prose is every line not beginning `#`, counted per file and summed. Batch total 27,803, average 1,853, inside the 26,000–28,000 band.** Per chapter: 946 1,830 · 947 1,931 · 948 1,725 · 949 1,758 · 950 1,792 · 951 1,993 · 952 1,845 · 953 1,830 · 954 1,730 · 955 1,916 · 956 1,813 · 957 1,755 · 958 1,895 · 959 1,970 · 960 2,020. **Collision sweep run before closing: no lighter figure in 946–960 ends in twenty-two followed by *days*, and `grep -oi 'twenty-two days'` over the fifteen files is 0.** One was written and rewritten: ch 960's *a lamp in her left hand that had not been lit in twenty-two days* was changed to *that had not carried a flame since the fourteenth of the month beginning three thousand and seventy-four*. **Every one of the fifteen lighter figures (1,525 through 1,563) was checked spoken-out for that string and none of them produces it**, which is a different result from ch 945's, where the flat 1,522 did.

**No tenth stage, no Last Ascension, no new cuts, no number, no announcement of her, no peak re-taken.** No frame scene, no eleventh. **Nobody dies, on the page or off it (D5)** — the only `dead` on the page is a lamp. No opening of the room, the bar, the bundle, the cage lamp or the cells; **the tower is left at ch 946 and is not on the page again**; the second place is never sat in; finding-day, the wear gap and the fair-hand/third-hand question are untouched and unraised. No em dashes, no bold, no italics, no tables, blockquotes, code fences or rules, no straight quotes or apostrophes, no Arabic numeral, no named weekday, month or week. No `Chapter N` and no sentence pointing at a volume, a chapter or a page. **All retired phrases are zero, including `nobody asked`, which was caught in a section heading at ch 956 and renamed.**

**Three chapters open on the morning figures — 948, 954, 959 — and no two of them are consecutive.** Ch 947 speaks the figures inside a scene. Fifteen different ending shapes; none of them bed or neutral action; none restating its own opening. Paragraphs beginning `She said` run **39 in 826 body paragraphs**, about one in twenty-one and well inside the one-in-six ceiling.

**A conflict inside `outline/batches/volume-16-batch-0004.md`, recorded and not edited.** Its ch 954 cell says *there are now three hundred and ninety-nine of them* and its ch 959 cell says *three hundred and ninety-eight so far*. **The two are mutually exclusive**, and both sit inside a run of five chapters, and the outline's own table fixes *the four hundred reached at ch 960* — which cannot be true if the count is already at three hundred and ninety-nine at 954. **The outline wins, as `outline/volume-16.md` line 461 and `outline/ending.md` §1 both fix the four hundred at ch 960, and the prose runs a count that is strictly increasing and reaches four hundred on the nineteenth of the month at about the seventh hour.** The card's figures are therefore not on the page and the two cells are left standing in the outline for the phase that owns it, exactly as Batch 0003 left the ch 941 phrase standing in the same file.

### The one place the card had to be read against the page, and why

**The card says the tower is left behind at ch 946 and never returned to, and the page agrees. The card also says Lu An is below and not on this page until the last chapter of it, and the page agrees, and no chapter in ch 946–960 mentions him, his doorway, or his oil.** She goes down eight flights and a stair and out of the other of two doors because the weather comes in under one of them, and there is no reason on earth for her to be in the wind on the morning she walks out of a building for the last time.

**This is a derivation table and not a spoken-figures table.** The bar row is **0 of 15**: she puts her hand flat on the bar once, at ch 946, on her way out, and takes no weight on it, and no chapter hands it a count. The cage-lamp row is spoken once, at ch 948, as a figure about a lamp she is never going to see out again. The room row is spoken at 947, 948, 951, 953, 954 and 959, and at 948, 951 and 953 it is spoken as **there was no room**, which is the batch's largest single change and is the point of it.

**The interval from the refusal at day 3,040 is not spoken anywhere in ch 946–960, deliberately.** Batch 0003 spent fifteen chapters on it and the state file for that batch recorded it as a live series. **This batch drops it out of the morning block and does not say that it has dropped it**, which is the permitted way to work with an absence. **A drafter of Batch 0005 must not reintroduce it as though fifteen chapters had not passed.**

**Fourteen elapsed-day figures were drafted wrong on the first pass and every one was corrected against its own anchor before closing**, and the finding is recorded because the class of error is the one this run has been repairing since ch 764: *four days of questions* at ch 955 and *seven days of nothing* (both were a fortnight) · *a lamp not lit in six days* at ch 951, *eight days* at ch 952, *sixteen days* at ch 957 and *twenty days* at ch 959 (all four are *since the fourteenth of the month beginning three thousand and seventy-four*) · *twelve days of questions in the open air* at ch 956 (eleven) · *nine days of working the queue* at ch 957 (ten) · *two hundred and forty times in nine days* at ch 957 (two hundred and seventy in ten) · *ten days out of a room* at ch 957 (twenty-eight) · *ninety-nine on the fourth of the month* at ch 957 (sixty-eight on the first and a hundred and forty-nine by the fifth hour of the fourth) · *eleven days* and *twelve days* at ch 958 and ch 959 (thirteen and eighteen) · *four words to five people in nine days* at ch 954 (eleven) · *five days of doing this* at ch 953 (ten) · *three days of not being able to get down it* at ch 948 (six) · *four days ago* at ch 960 (four, after the last binding was two days earlier than first written).

**That last sentence of that paragraph — *every morning figure was checked against the table above after the prose was final, and all fifteen chapters' spoken figures re-derive* — was false when it was written, and the batch's review found it to be false. It is corrected here and the finding is recorded under *VOLUME 16 BATCH 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR* at the foot of this file. The claim had been made about the figures in the morning blocks and it was true of those; it was not true of the figures said inside scenes, and nine spoken figures inside scenes did not re-derive.**

### What Batch 0004 inherits

**He is in no chapter from ch 961 on and never again, and the shape of the silence was named once at ch 945 and may not be named again.** She is alone on a road in bad weather with four hundred people in her arithmetic, a lamp she cannot refill, a book of eight leaves of which one is now written on in charcoal, and **a sentence she has not found and does not know how to look for.** **She does not know that the channel is hers alone and is not to find out before ch 975.** The layer keeps the walls, holds the four hundred, has said nothing to her since the second hour of the twenty-seventh, and has one bracketed line left in the whole book at ch 971. **She has said once, out loud, that she does not know what the four hundred is costing her, and no later card may improve on it, soften it or answer it.** `nobody` has 1 of its volume line of 30 left and `eleven` has 17 of 40; `forgiv` is 0 in the volume and its one is at ch 1000; `thank` is 0 and its stem is 1 across the whole volume and must stay 1; **brackets have one left, at ch 971, and nothing after it.**

## VOLUME 16 BATCH 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR, 15 Oct 2026. Nine findings, no chapter restarted, no plot moved

**The reviewer's run was cut off before it could write a verdict: the check commands it had issued are all in `logs/volume-16-batch-0004.review.log`, and the last thing in that file is a rejected shell call, not a finding. The checks that did complete came back clean — `court`/`ledger`/`choir`/`ascendant`/`forgiv`/`thank`/`the year`/`twenty-two days` all 0, `grep -oi 'thank[a-z]*'` 0, no banned construction, no markup, no straight quote or apostrophe, no em dash, longest paragraph 92, fifteen unique titles, one bracketed line at ch 955, word counts 27,623 average 1,842 as recorded. The repair below was done against the committed prose and the finished checks, and it changed no beat, no day, no title, no midpoint, no climax, no bracket and no chapter's ending.** The batch's own claim that all fifteen chapters' spoken figures re-derived was false, and the figures said inside scenes had not been checked, only the morning blocks.

**Twenty-four findings across nine chapters, and no chapter was restarted.** Eight of them are the same class the batch's own closing note claimed to have swept, and the reason is now written down where it belongs: **a figure checked against its anchor in the morning block is not a figure checked.** Every wrong figure above sat inside a scene and had been carried from a neighbouring chapter rather than derived.

The eleven are still spoken once. She still does not know what the four hundred costs her and still said so out loud once. He is still in no chapter of the fifteen in any form. The bracket budget is unchanged: one spent at ch 955, one left at ch 971.

**Rejected after checking, and why. Four of the twenty-three.**

- *ch 947's 792 and 785 should be 793 and 786*, on the ground that the second morning is the chapter's own day. It is not: the chapter's third day is the fourteenth and the figures are spoken on the fourteenth at 786, so the second morning is the thirteenth, which gives 792 and 785, and the second night is the same night. **All three figures were checked against each other before they were checked against the table and they agree.** The finding assumed a day the chapter does not use.
- *He Yu should have been at the end of that road five days, not four.* The house counts an interval as the subtraction, exclusive, from the true anchor, and says *counting both ends* when it means the inclusive figure. The fifteenth is the anchor; four days is the subtraction.
- *ch 960 should end on the four hundred.* The irreversible beat lands and the chapter then spends what it cost, and the last line is a decision with a question in it. Moving it would cut the cost off the climax.
- *ch 948's last paragraph repeats itself.* It is the chapter's turn stated twice, and the last line is a need with nothing in her that can meet it, which is the ending shape and not a repetition of it.

**One thing this pass found that is not repaired here and is not a Batch 0004 defect. The hands.** Batch 0004 says throughout that the **left** hand has three fingers and a thumb and is the hand that writes, holds the lamp and shakes, and that the right arm is open at the elbow. Volumes 1 to 16, including ch 903, 908, 919 and 920 of this volume, say **three fingers and a thumb on each**, and the early volumes also say **four fingers and a thumb on the right hand**. The manuscript has carried both for a long time. Batch 0004's own usage is internally consistent and its one contradiction is fixed at ch 951 above. **The canon question is recorded in `state/open-threads.md` as an open item for the Volume 16 close, and no batch rewrites forty-five chapters of a prior batch's phrasing to settle it.**

### What the sentence is, and where it is, and where it is not

**The sentence, fixed by `outline/ending.md` §4 and drafted here out of her own arithmetic on a flat in sleet at ch 968: *Never chosen. I was told. There is a difference.*** **Nine words, and the count that lands on the page is nine and the outline's nine is not corrected.** It is said standing, in the ordinary voice, first in her own voice, before it is a line anywhere, at about the ninth hour on the thirtieth of the month beginning three thousand and one hundred and thirty-four, in front of **eight** people, at a gap in a low wall at a crossing.

**It is not in a book, a register, a board, a wall, a document or a memory of somebody's words, and it is not written on any of the eight leaves.** The book is against her fourth rib through the whole batch and comes out twice — at ch 966, where she decides with a stub of charcoal in her hand not to put the sentence on a leaf, and at ch 971, where she looks at the two leaves that are still blank. The one thing that was written on a leaf at all remains the three sentences of ch 950, put there in charcoal about four weeks ago and never read.

**She said it once before she said it properly**, at ch 969, out in a fog on the thirteenth of the month, to an empty place, and nothing came, and she tried to withdraw it in the open afterwards and could not, which is ch 958's finding applied to her own mouth. **She worked out at ch 968 why it might have to be in that shape and not another one**: a keeping reports and does not decide, so a refusal has to be a report, and the only true report she has about herself that is also a refusal is that she was told and not chosen. **She found at ch 963 that there is not one line in nine hundred years saying that keeping chose anybody, and that she has never once appeared in the record she has read every morning since she was seven.**

### What ch 975 is and is not

**It is not a victory, not a triumph, no crowd assembled, no four hundred arranged, no cheer, no light, no warmth, and no sense that the argument was won.** The argument was had at ch 899 and is not re-litigated. Eight people come because a market is on that day and they had heard the word, and she chose a market day **at ch 972 precisely so that the day would fill itself without her having to organise it**, which is the plainest place she can stand. She puts the arithmetic first, in the plainest words she has, including that not one person can say no to it now and that she does not know what it does to them.

**What comes down on the page at ch 975 is nothing.** The ground under her boots goes quiet the way it goes quiet when the keeping takes a thing, and then — the only new thing in the world — **the quiet does not stop**, for the rest of the hour, and she has to keep shifting her weight. **The open place behind her fourth rib is not there.** Skin, the ordinary kind, with nothing under it, where a place the size of her thumbnail had been since she was a child. **And she does not say it out loud, standing, in the ordinary voice, because she cannot derive what it means** — which is the last time in the book the fourth term is bent, and it is bent by refusing to say a thing rather than by saying it.

**The fall begins in the hour ch 975 ends.** It is not named, not announced, not bracketed, and nothing is visible of it: the rain stops in the tenth hour, eight people go quiet at once, a dog stops in the road, and a carter with an empty cart comes up from the flat, turns round and goes back down it. **She does not know it has begun and no card in this batch is permitted to let her know.** Day 3,164 at ch 976 is the first of a month and the first of the fall and is the next batch's first chapter.

### What Batch 0005 inherits, and what may not be re-opened

Inherited as fact and unchanged: the four hundred exist and cannot be un-bound · **she still does not know what the four hundred costs her and said so out loud once at ch 960 and no chapter of this batch improves on it** · the offer is withdrawn and the register is on a table at the top of a mountain she is not going back to · the layer keeps the walls, obeys, and has said its last word in the book · **she does not know that the channel is hers alone** · the sentence is said and cannot be un-said · the open place behind her fourth rib is gone and she has not derived what that means · a lamp she cannot refill, carried in her left hand, dark since the fourteenth of the month beginning three thousand and seventy-four · a book of eight leaves against her fourth rib with one of the four blank ones written on in charcoal and two of them still blank · `nobody` **1 of 30 left in the whole volume** · `eleven` **already 14 over the line of 40, and 8 of the 14 are this batch's** · brackets **0 left, and none anywhere in ch 972–1000** · the `thank` stem at 1 across the volume and it must stay 1.

May not be re-opened: that the sentence was a victory or that she was brave · that the layer argued, fought, retaliated, acknowledged or explained anything · that the walls came down on the page at ch 975 · that anybody knows what has happened · that anybody died · that the bearer was told it was the last bundle — **he was not, and nobody ever tells him, and ch 965 is the only page he is on** · that the blank leaves are where the sentence is · that the four hundred assembled · that anybody thanked anybody, or that she was thanked · that the eleven before her are named or counted again before ch 1000 · that the shape of the silence is described a second time.
